title: 7 stripes tartan setts (page 3)
date: 2023-03-18

1,559 setts across 1,375 tartans with 7 stripes, grouped by colour-sequence pattern. Patterns with fewer than 40 setts are listed in full below; busier ones link to their own page. Each variant shows ΔT — its mean distance (OKLab ΔE) from the pattern's base colours — nearest first, with a stripe bar in the nearest human-palette colour names (hover a stripe to read it).

| 0.11 | <span style="display:inline-flex;width:220px;height:1.1em;vertical-align:middle;border:1px solid #0003"><span title="Grey" style="width:14.0px;background:#636363"></span><span title="K" style="width:31.4px;background:#ccc"></span><span title="Grey" style="width:27.9px;background:#636363"></span><span title="Green" style="width:10.5px;background:#008B2A"></span><span title="Orange" style="width:10.5px;background:#A65C11"></span><span title="White" style="width:97.8px;background:#F7F7F7"></span><span title="Grey" style="width:27.9px;background:#636363"></span></span> | [MacTavish of Dunardry Dress Clan Tartan Tartan Number: 10731. Earliest known date: 6 November 2012 A 'new' MacTavish Clan tartan, re-creating the Dunardry tartan known to Isobel, daughter of MacLachlan of that Ilk, paternal grandmother to Sheriff Dugald MacTavish of Dunardry. Evidence for the existence of this design can be found in a letter from Sheriff Dugald MacTavish at Kilchrist to John MacTavish, 18 February 1845, the original of which is held in the National Archives of Canada (Library and Archives Canada, James Hargrave and family fonds MG19-A21, Series 2 Mactavish family papers, volume 25/2, “D.M. to John McTavish. Regarding Scottish costume for son John, who will become the lineal representative of the Thane of Knapdale”, February 18th 1845, Kilchrist). The letter describes a tartan that ‘should have as much white as there was yellow in the MacLachlans tartan’. The registration of this Clan tartan has been approved by the current Clan Chief. Registrant details: Steven MacTavish of Dunardry, 27th Chief Clan MacTavish P.O. Box 93093, Burlington, Ontario, Canada, L7M 4A3 See products available Copyright © Blair Urquhart, Comrie, 2015](/setts/s7/b8w28r3g3b8k9b4~b5a6e90-g487731-k000000-r95765b-wffffff~x2/) | house-of-tartan |
